         cheques.wentto_bankstrans ,           cheques.cheqvalue ,           cheques.bankcode2 ,           cheques.refuse_reason ,           cheques.refuse_charges ,           cheques.cheqvaluediscount ,           cheques.cheqvaluetax ,           cheques.cheqvaluedamgha ,           cheques.trans_time ,           original_refuse_reason = cheques.refuse_reason,cheques.payrefusesno ,           cheques.phcode ,           cheques.cheqcashfees ,           cheques.open_trans ,           cheques.cheqno ,           cheques.handedto ,           cheques.creditcardtype ,           cheques.branchcode ,           cheques.ptcode ,           cheques.bcurrency ,           cheques.bcrate ,           cheqvaluediscount_lc=round( cheqvaluediscount * bcrate , 2 ),           cheqvaluetax_lc=round( cheqvaluetax * bcrate , 2 ),           cheqvaluedamgha_lc=round( cheqvaluedamgha * bcrate , 2 ),           cheques.cheqvalue_lc ,           cheques.banknewbal_lc ,           cheques.personnewbal ,           cheques.banknewbal ,           banktype = 0,           cheques.banktrans_time ,           cheques.ourcheqsnop ,           cheques.tf_branchcode ,           cheques.tf_bankcode ,           cheques.tf_cheqsno ,           cheques.bcrate_std ,           localcustomers.branchcustname ,           cheques.personcode ,           cheques.origdoc ,           cheques.blockinv ,cheques.cheqvalueother ,           cheqvalueother_lc=round( cheqvalueother * bcrate , 2 ),           cheques.custbranchcode ,           customerbranches.custbranchname ,           cheques.origintaxp  FROM cheques LEFT OUTER JOIN localcustomers ON cheques.phcode = localcustomers.phcode LEFT OUTER JOIN customerbranches ON cheques.personcode = customerbranches.custcode AND cheques.custbranchcode = customerbranches.custbranchcode     WHERE ( cheques.branchcode = '150' ) And          ( cheques.cheqsno = 70292)  ORDER BY cheques.cheqsno          ASC

SELECT stktrans.doccode , stktrans.docnumber , stktrans.transqty , stktrans.newqty , stktrans.docdate , branches.branchname , stktrans.bonusqty , stktrans.trans_time , stktrans.transprice , stktrans.newcostprice , stktrans.transprice_total , stores.storename , stktrans.pharmacydiscp , stktrans.usercode , stktrans.dblitemflag , costcenters.costcenterdescr , items.itemtrans , personsdata.personname , stktrans.itemexpirydate , stktransm.phcode , stktrans.item_partno , stktrans.storecode FROM stktrans LEFT OUTER JOIN personsdata ON stktrans.personcode = personsdata.personcode LEFT OUTER JOIN branches ON stktrans.personcode = branches.branchcode LEFT OUTER JOIN stores ON stktrans.personcode = stores.storecode LEFT OUTER JOIN costcenters ON stktrans.costcentercode = costcenters.costcentercode , items , stktransm WHERE ( stktrans.itemcode = items.itemcode ) and ( stktrans.branchcode = stktransm.branchcode ) and ( stktrans.doccode = stktransm.doccode ) and ( stktrans.docnumber = stktransm.docnumber ) and ( stktrans.docdate = stktransm.docdate ) and ( ( 1 = 1 ) ) and (stktrans.itemcode = '404' ) and (stktrans.storecode = '100' ) and (stktrans.docdate >= convert(datetime, '2019/1/1', 111) ) and (stktrans.docdate <= convert(datetime, '2026/7/3', 111) ) ORDER BY stktrans.trans_time ASC, stktrans.dblitemflag ASC
